> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1	# reigh-workspace
2	
3	Development workspace for the Reigh video generation platform. Clone this repo, then clone the three component repos into it to get a fully working environment.
4	
5	## Setup
6	
7	```bash
8	# 1. Clone the workspace
9	git clone https://github.com/banodoco/reigh-workspace.git
10	cd reigh-workspace
11	
12	# 2. Clone the component repos
13	git clone https://github.com/banodoco/reigh-app.git
14	git clone https://github.com/banodoco/reigh-worker.git
15	git clone https://github.com/banodoco/reigh-worker-orchestrator.git
16	```
17	
18	Your workspace should look like this:
19	
20	```
21	reigh-workspace/
22	  reigh-app/                  Frontend (React/Vite) + Supabase edge functions
23	  reigh-worker/               GPU worker (Python, runs on RunPod)
24	  reigh-worker-orchestrator/  Worker scaling + API task dispatch (runs on Railway)
25	  structure.md                System-wide architecture overview
26	  docs/                       Debugging, credentials, and deep-dives
27	```
28	
29	## Repo Setup
30	
31	Each repo has its own dependencies:
32	
33	**reigh-app** (frontend):
34	```bash
35	cd reigh-app
36	cp .env.example .env          # Fill in Supabase credentials
37	npm install
38	npm run dev                   # http://localhost:2222
39	```
40	
41	**reigh-worker** (GPU worker):
42	```bash
43	cd reigh-worker
44	cp .env.example .env          # Fill in Supabase + RunPod credentials
45	python -m venv venv
46	source venv/bin/activate
47	pip install -r requirements.txt
48	python worker.py --debug
49	```
50	
51	**reigh-worker-orchestrator** (control plane):
52	```bash
53	cd reigh-worker-orchestrator
54	cp env.example .env            # Fill in Supabase + RunPod + Railway credentials
55	pip install -r gpu_orchestrator/requirements.txt
56	pip install -r api_orchestrator/requirements.txt
57	python -m gpu_orchestrator.main status
58	```
59	
60	## Key Docs
61	
62	| Doc | Purpose |
63	|-----|---------|
64	| [structure.md](structure.md) | How the three repos fit together — architecture, data flow, database schema |
65	| [docs/debugging.md](docs/debugging.md) | When something breaks — decision table, debug tools, blast radius |
66	| [docs/credentials.md](docs/credentials.md) | Where to get every API key — points to each repo's `.env` file |
67	
68	## Architecture
69	
70	```
71	User → reigh-app (frontend)
72	         ↓ create-task edge function
73	       Supabase (DB + Edge Functions + Storage + Realtime)
74	         ↓ claim-next-task
75	       reigh-worker (GPU on RunPod)
76	         ↓ complete_task
77	       Supabase → Realtime → reigh-app (video appears)
78	
79	       reigh-worker-orchestrator (Railway)
80	         monitors demand → scales workers up/down
81	         dispatches API tasks (fal.ai, Wavespeed)
82	```
83
```

1	# Reigh: System Structure
2	
3	> **How to Use This Guide**
4	> - Start here to understand how the three repos fit together
5	> - Follow links to sub-docs in [docs/structure_detail/](docs/structure_detail/) for deep dives
6	> - Each repo also has its own README/STRUCTURE.md for repo-specific detail
7	> - Source of truth is always the code — this guide points you in the right direction
8	
9	---
10	
11	## System Overview
12	
13	Reigh is a video generation platform. Users create tasks in the frontend, GPU workers process them, and the orchestrator manages scaling.
14	
15	```
16	┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
17	│                    Reigh-Collection                      │
18	├──────────────────┬──────────────────┬───────────────────┤
19	│     reigh-app/       │  reigh-worker/   │  reigh-worker-    │
20	│                  │                  │  Orchestrator/    │
21	│  React/Vite UI   │  Python GPU      │  Python control   │
22	│  Supabase edge   │  worker on       │  plane on         │
23	│  functions       │  RunPod pods     │  Railway          │
24	└────────┬─────────┴────────┬─────────┴─────────┬─────────┘
25	         │                  │                   │
26	         └──────────────────┼───────────────────┘
27	                            │
28	                    ┌───────┴───────┐
29	                    │   Supabase    │
30	                    │  Postgres DB  │
31	                    │  Edge Funcs   │
32	                    │  Storage      │
33	                    │  Realtime     │
34	                    └───────────────┘
35	```
36	
37	---
38	
39	## Tech Stack
40	
41	| Layer | Technology | Repo |
42	|-------|------------|------|
43	| **Frontend** | React + Vite + TypeScript | reigh-app/ |
44	| **Styling** | TailwindCSS + shadcn-ui | reigh-app/ |
45	| **Backend** | Supabase (Postgres + Edge Functions + Storage + Realtime) | reigh-app/ (edge functions + migrations) |
46	| **GPU Workers** | Python + PyTorch + CUDA | reigh-worker/ |
47	| **Video Engine** | Wan2GP (vendored) | reigh-worker/Wan2GP/ |
48	| **Orchestration** | Python (GPU scaling + API task dispatch) | reigh-worker-orchestrator/ |
49	| **GPU Hosting** | RunPod | Managed by Orchestrator + Arnold |
50	| **Orchestrator Hosting** | Railway | reigh-worker-orchestrator/ |
51	| **External APIs** | fal.ai, Wavespeed (image editing) | Dispatched by Orchestrator |
52	
53	---
54	
55	## End-to-End Data Flow
56	
57	```
58	User clicks Generate
59	  → Frontend builds payload                           reigh-app/src/shared/lib/tasks/
60	  → Calls create-task edge function                   reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/
61	  → Row inserted in tasks table (status: Queued)      Supabase Postgres
62	  → Worker polls claim-next-task                      reigh-app/supabase/functions/claim-next-task/
63	  → Worker processes task on GPU                      reigh-worker/worker.py
64	  → Worker calls complete_task edge function           reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete_task/
65	  → DB trigger creates generation row                 Supabase
66	  → Realtime broadcasts to UI                         reigh-app/src/ (React Query subscription)
67	  → Video appears in gallery
68	```
69	
70	**Pipelines** (e.g., video travel): Orchestrator creates parent task → spawns child segment tasks → each child follows the flow above → stitch task joins outputs.
71	
72	**API tasks** (e.g., image editing): API Orchestrator claims task → dispatches to fal.ai/Wavespeed → writes result back.
73	
74	---
75	
76	## Repo Structure
77	
78	### reigh-app/ — Frontend + Edge Functions
79	
80	The main application. React SPA + Supabase serverless backend.
81	
82	| Path | Purpose |
83	|------|---------|
84	| `src/app/` | App bootstrap & routing |
85	| `src/pages/` | Top-level pages (Home, Shots, Art, Share) |
86	| `src/tools/` | Feature modules — each tool: `pages/`, `components/`, `hooks/`, `settings.ts` |
87	| `src/domains/` | Domain logic (billing, lora, media-lightbox, generation) |
88	| `src/features/` | Feature slices (tasks, shots, gallery, resources, projects, settings) |
89	| `src/shared/` | Cross-domain primitives, UI components, contracts |
90	| `src/integrations/` | Supabase client, auth, realtime, instrumentation |
91	| `supabase/functions/` | Edge Functions (task lifecycle, payments, AI) |
92	| `supabase/migrations/` | DB schema migrations |
93	| `scripts/debug.py` | Debug CLI for tasks, pipelines, logs, pods |
94	
95	Full detail: [reigh-app/README.md](./reigh-app/README.md)
96	
97	### reigh-worker/ — GPU Worker
98	
99	Queue-based video generation system built on Wan2GP.
100

### Key Findings

**No direct matches found** - Neither UUID appears in:
- Log files (.log files across reigh-app, reigh-worker, reigh-worker-orchestrator)
- Source code files (.py, .ts, .tsx, .js files)

- JSON data files (megaplan, desloppify, package configs)

- Any text, CSV, or database files

### Project Structure Overview

This is the **Reigh workspace** - a video generation platform with three components:

1. **reigh-app/** - React/Vite frontend + Supabase edge functions (task creation, lifecycle)
2. **reigh-worker/** - Python GPU worker on RunPod (video generation processing)
3. **reigh-worker-orchestrator/** - Python control plane on Railway (GPU scaling, API task dispatch)

Tasks are stored in a Supabase Postgres database, with task IDs as UUIDs.

### Files Checked

- Log files: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-worker-orchestrator/orchestrator.log` (419 lines, no UUID matches)
- Output directories: `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/outputs/` (contains video files, no logs with UUIDs)
- All .desloppify and .megaplan directories (planning/codebase health documents)
- Root orchestrator.log (empty/single line)
- All localhost*.log files in reigh-app

### Conclusion

These UUIDs are not stored locally in the workspace directory. They likely exist only in the remote **Supabase Postgres database**. To find logs or output related to these tasks, you would need to:
1. Query the Supabase `tasks` table directly
2. Check Supabase storage buckets
3. Review the Supabase logs/activity
4. Check Railway logs for the orchestrator if tasks were dispatched there

18		"name" text NOT NULL,

22		"settings" jsonb
23	);
24	
25	-- Create tasks table

29		"params" jsonb NOT NULL,
30		"status" "task_status" DEFAULT 'Queued' NOT NULL,
31		"dependant_on" uuid,

34		"updated_at" timestamp with time zone,
35		"project_id" uuid NOT NULL,
36		"generation_processed_at" timestamp with time zone
37	);
38	
39	-- Create generations table
40	CREATE TABLE "generations" (
41		"id" uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
42		"tasks" jsonb,
43		"params" jsonb,
44		"location" text,
45		"type" text,
46		"created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
47		"updated_at" timestamp with time zone,
48		"project_id" uuid NOT NULL
49	);
50	
51	-- Create shots table
52	CREATE TABLE "shots" (
53		"id" uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() NOT NULL,
54		"name" text NOT NULL,
55		"created_at" timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now() NOT NULL,
56		"updated_at" timestamp with time zone,
57		"project_id" uuid NOT NULL,
58		"settings" jsonb
59	);
60	
61	-- Create shot_generations join table

64		"shot_id" uuid NOT NULL,
65		"generation_id" uuid NOT NULL,

67	);

73		"type" text NOT NULL,

76	);

80	ALTER TABLE "tasks" ADD CONSTRAINT "tasks_project_id_projects_id_fk" FOREIGN KEY ("project_id") REFERENCES "public"."projects"("id") ON DELETE cascade ON UPDATE no action;

31	  enable_upscale: boolean;
32	  interpolation?: FilmInterpolationApiParams;

34	  shot_id?: string;
35	  based_on?: string;
36	  source_variant_id?: string;
37	}

40	
41	interface UseVideoEnhanceProps {
42	  projectId: string | undefined;
43	  videoUrl: string | undefined;
44	  shotId?: string;
45	  /** Generation ID to create variant on (for based_on relationship) */
46	  generationId?: string;
47	  /** Active variant ID - track which variant was enhanced */
48	  activeVariantId?: string | null;
49	  /** Settings from persistence system */
50	  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
51	  /** Setter for updating settings */
52	  updateSettings: (updates: Partial<VideoEnhanceSettings>) => void;
53	}
54	
55	interface UseVideoEnhanceReturn {
56	  // Settings passed through for convenience
57	  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
58	  updateSetting: <K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
59	    key: K,
60	    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
61	  ) => void;
62	
63	  // Validation
64	  isValid: boolean;
65	  canSubmit: boolean;
66	
67	  // Actions
68	  handleGenerate: () => Promise<void>;
69	
70	  // Status
71	  isGenerating: boolean;
72	  generateSuccess: boolean;
73	}
74	
75	/**
76	 * Hook for managing video enhancement actions
77	 *
78	 * Settings are managed externally via the persistence system
79	 * This hook handles validation and task creation
80	 */
81	export function useVideoEnhance({
82	  projectId,
83	  videoUrl,
84	  shotId,
85	  generationId,
86	  activeVariantId,
87	  settings,
88	  updateSettings,
89	}: UseVideoEnhanceProps): UseVideoEnhanceReturn {
90	  const [isGenerating, setIsGenerating] = useState(false);
91	  const [generateSuccess, setGenerateSuccess] = useState(false);
92	  const run = useTaskPlaceholder();
93	
94	  // Adapter to match the expected interface
95	  const updateSetting = useCallback(<K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
96	    key: K,
97	    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
98	  ) => {
99	    updateSettings({ [key]: value });
100	    // Reset success state when settings change
101	    setGenerateSuccess(false);
102	  }, [updateSettings]);
103	
104	  // Validation: at least one mode must be enabled
105	  const isValid = settings.enableInterpolation || settings.enableUpscale;
106	
107	  // Can submit: valid settings + required data
108	  const canSubmit = isValid && !!projectId && !!videoUrl && !isGenerating;
109	
110	  // Handle generate action
111	  const handleGenerate = useCallback(async () => {
112	    if (!canSubmit || !projectId || !videoUrl) {
113	      return;
114	    }
115	
116	    setIsGenerating(true);
117	    setGenerateSuccess(false);
118	
119	    const enhanceLabel = [
120	      settings.enableInterpolation && 'Interpolation',
121	      settings.enableUpscale && 'Upscale',
122	    ].filter(Boolean).join(' + ');
123	
124	    try {
125	      await run({
126	        taskType: 'video_enhance',
127	        label: enhanceLabel || 'Video Enhance',
128	        context: 'useVideoEnhance',
129	        toastTitle: 'Failed to create video enhancement task',
130	        create: () => {
131	          const params: VideoEnhanceTaskParams = {
132	            project_id: projectId,
133	            video_url: videoUrl,
134	            enable_interpolation: settings.enableInterpolation,
135	            enable_upscale: settings.enableUpscale,
136	            shot_id: shotId,
137	            based_on: generationId,
138	            source_variant_id: activeVariantId || undefined,
139	          };
140	
141	          if (settings.enableInterpolation) {
142	            params.interpolation = {
143	              num_frames: settings.numFrames,
144	              use_calculated_fps: true,
145	            };
146	          }
147	
148	          if (settings.enableUpscale) {
149	            params.upscale = {
150	              upscale_factor: settings.upscaleFactor,
151	              color_fix: settings.colorFix,
152	              output_quality: settings.outputQuality,
153	            };
154	          }
155	
156	          const { project_id, ...input } = params;
157	          return createTask({
158	            project_id,
159	            family: 'video_enhance',
160	            input,
161	          });
162	        },
163	        onSuccess: () => {
164	          setGenerateSuccess(true);
165	        },
166	      });
167	    } finally {
168	      setIsGenerating(false);
169	    }
170	  }, [
171	    canSubmit,
172	    projectId,
173	    videoUrl,
174	    shotId,
175	    generationId,
176	    activeVariantId,
177	    settings,
178	    run,
179	  ]);
180	
181	  return {
182	    settings,
183	    updateSetting,
184	    isValid,
185	    canSubmit,
186	    handleGenerate,
187	    isGenerating,
188	    generateSuccess,
189	  };
190	}
191

1	/**
2	 * VideoEnhanceForm Component
3	 *
4	 * Form for video enhancement settings including:
5	 * - Frame interpolation (FILM)
6	 * - Video upscaling (FlashVSR)
7	 *
8	 * Toggle switches reveal settings for each enhancement type.
9	 * Submit is disabled if neither mode is enabled.
10	 */
11	
12	import React, { useEffect, useState, useRef } from 'react';
13	import { Button } from '@/shared/components/ui/button';
14	import { Label } from '@/shared/components/ui/primitives/label';
15	import { Switch } from '@/shared/components/ui/switch';
16	import { Slider } from '@/shared/components/ui/slider';
17	import {
18	  Select,
19	  SelectContent,
20	  SelectItem,
21	  SelectTrigger,
22	  SelectValue,
23	} from '@/shared/components/ui/select';
24	import { Loader2, Check, Sparkles, Film, ArrowUp } from 'lucide-react';
25	import { cn } from '@/shared/components/ui/contracts/cn';
26	import type { VideoEnhanceSettings } from '../hooks/useGenerationEditSettings';
27	
28	// Default FPS for AI-generated videos (matches useVideoEditing assumption)
29	const DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS = 16;
30	
31	/**
32	 * Detect video FPS using requestVideoFrameCallback
33	 * Returns null while detecting, then the detected FPS
34	 */
35	function useDetectVideoFps(videoUrl: string | undefined): number | null {
36	  const [detectedFps, setDetectedFps] = useState<number | null>(null);
37	  const videoRef = useRef<HTMLVideoElement | null>(null);
38	
39	  useEffect(() => {
40	    if (!videoUrl) return;
41	
42	    // Create a hidden video element to detect FPS
43	    const video = document.createElement('video');
44	    video.src = videoUrl;
45	    video.muted = true;
46	    video.playsInline = true;
47	    videoRef.current = video;
48	
49	    let frameCount = 0;
50	    let startTime = 0;
51	    let rafId: number | null = null;
52	
53	    const countFrame = (_now: number, metadata: VideoFrameCallbackMetadata) => {
54	      if (frameCount === 0) {
55	        // First frame - just record start time, don't count it
56	        startTime = metadata.mediaTime;
57	        frameCount++;
58	        rafId = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(countFrame);
59	        return;
60	      }
61	      frameCount++;
62	
63	      // After ~0.5 seconds of video time, calculate FPS
64	      // frameCount-1 because we don't count the first frame (it's our reference point)
65	      const elapsed = metadata.mediaTime - startTime;
66	      if (elapsed >= 0.5 && frameCount > 2) {
67	        const fps = Math.round((frameCount - 1) / elapsed);
68	        setDetectedFps(fps);
69	        video.pause();
70	        return;
71	      }
72	
73	      // Continue counting
74	      rafId = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(countFrame);
75	    };
76	
77	    video.addEventListener('loadedmetadata', () => {
78	      // Start playing to count frames
79	      if ('requestVideoFrameCallback' in video) {
80	        rafId = video.requestVideoFrameCallback(countFrame);
81	        video.play().catch(() => {
82	          // If autoplay blocked, fall back to default
83	          setDetectedFps(DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS);
84	        });
85	      } else {
86	        // Browser doesn't support requestVideoFrameCallback, use default
87	        setDetectedFps(DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS);
88	      }
89	    });
90	
91	    video.addEventListener('error', () => {
92	      setDetectedFps(DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS);
93	    });
94	
95	    // Cleanup
96	    return () => {
97	      if (rafId !== null && videoRef.current) {
98	        // Note: cancelVideoFrameCallback may not exist in all browsers
99	        try {
100	          (videoRef.current as HTMLVideoElement & { cancelVideoFrameCallback?: (id: number) => void }).cancelVideoFrameCallback?.(rafId);
101	        } catch { /* intentionally ignored */ }
102	      }
103	      video.pause();
104	      video.src = '';
105	      videoRef.current = null;
106	    };
107	  }, [videoUrl]);
108	
109	  return detectedFps;
110	}
111	
112	interface VideoEnhanceFormProps {
113	  settings: VideoEnhanceSettings;
114	  onUpdateSetting: <K extends keyof VideoEnhanceSettings>(
115	    key: K,
116	    value: VideoEnhanceSettings[K]
117	  ) => void;
118	  onGenerate: () => void;
119	  isGenerating: boolean;
120	  generateSuccess: boolean;
121	  canSubmit: boolean;
122	  variant?: 'desktop' | 'mobile';
123	  /** Video URL for FPS detection */
124	  videoUrl?: string;
125	}
126	
127	export const VideoEnhanceForm: React.FC<VideoEnhanceFormProps> = ({
128	  settings,
129	  onUpdateSetting,
130	  onGenerate,
131	  isGenerating,
132	  generateSuccess,
133	  canSubmit,
134	  variant = 'desktop',
135	  videoUrl,
136	}) => {
137	  const isMobile = variant === 'mobile';
138	
139	  // Detect video FPS
140	  const detectedFps = useDetectVideoFps(videoUrl);
141	  const videoFps = detectedFps ?? DEFAULT_VIDEO_FPS;
142	
143	  // Calculate output FPS based on interpolation setting
144	  // FILM adds num_frames between each pair, so output FPS ≈ input * (num_frames + 1)
145	  const outputFps = Math.round(videoFps * (settings.numFrames + 1));
146	
147	  return (
148	    <div className={cn("flex flex-col gap-4", isMobile ? "px-3 py-2" : "p-4")}>
149	      {/* Interpolation Toggle & Settings */}
150	      <div className="space-y-3">
151	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
152	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
153	            <Film className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
154	            <Label htmlFor="enable-interpolation" className="font-medium">
155	              Frame Interpolation
156	            </Label>
157	          </div>
158	          <Switch
159	            id="enable-interpolation"
160	            checked={settings.enableInterpolation}
161	            onCheckedChange={(checked) =>
162	              onUpdateSetting('enableInterpolation', checked)
163	            }
164	          />
165	        </div>
166	
167	        {settings.enableInterpolation && (
168	          <div className="pl-6 space-y-3 border-l-2 border-muted ml-2">
169	            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
170	              Add frames between existing frames for smoother motion
171	            </p>
172	
173	            {/* Frames to interpolate slider */}
174	            <div className="space-y-2">
175	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
176	                <Label className="text-sm">Frames to add</Label>
177	                <span className="text-sm text-muted-foreground font-mono">
178	                  {settings.numFrames}x
179	                </span>
180	              </div>
181	              <Slider
182	                value={settings.numFrames}
183	                onValueChange={(value) =>
184	                  onUpdateSetting('numFrames', Array.isArray(value) ? (value[0] ?? settings.numFrames) : value)
185	                }
186	                min={1}
187	                max={4}
188	                step={1}
189	                className="w-full"
190	              />
191	              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
192	                {settings.numFrames + 1}x frame rate ({videoFps}fps → {outputFps}fps)
193	              </p>
194	            </div>
195	          </div>
196	        )}
197	      </div>
198	
199	      {/* Divider */}
200	      <div className="border-t border-border" />
201	
202	      {/* Upscale Toggle & Settings */}
203	      <div className="space-y-3">
204	        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
205	          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
206	            <ArrowUp className="h-4 w-4 text-muted-foreground" />
207	            <Label htmlFor="enable-upscale" className="font-medium">
208	              Upscale Resolution
209	            </Label>
210	          </div>
211	          <Switch

216	            }
217	          />
218	        </div>
219	
220	        {settings.enableUpscale && (
221	          <div className="pl-6 space-y-3 border-l-2 border-muted ml-2">
222	            <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
223	              Increase video resolution using AI upscaling
224	            </p>
225	
226	            {/* Upscale factor slider */}
227	            <div className="space-y-2">
228	              <div className="flex items-center justify-between">

231	                  {settings.upscaleFactor.toFixed(1)}x
232	                </span>
233	              </div>

238	                }
239	                min={1}

241	                step={0.1}
242	                className="w-full"

244	              <p className="text-xs text-muted-foreground">
245	                {settings.upscaleFactor <= 2 && '720p → 1440p (or similar)'}
246	                {settings.upscaleFactor > 2 && settings.upscaleFactor <= 3 && '720p → 2160p (or similar)'}
247	                {settings.upscaleFactor > 3 && '720p → 2880p (or similar)'}
248	              </p>
249	            </div>
250

80	/** Builds phase config with user LoRAs merged into each phase. */
81	export function buildPhaseConfigWithLoras(
82	  userLoras: PathLoraConfig[] = [],
83	  baseConfig: PhaseConfig = DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG
84	): PhaseConfig {
85	  if (userLoras.length === 0) {
86	    return baseConfig;
87	  }
88	  
89	  const additionalLoras = userLoras
90	    .filter(lora => lora.path)
91	    .map(lora => ({
92	      url: lora.path,
93	      multiplier: lora.strength.toFixed(2)
94	    }));
95	
96	  return {
97	    ...baseConfig,
98	    phases: baseConfig.phases.map(phase => ({
99	      ...phase,
100	      loras: [...phase.loras, ...additionalLoras]
101	    }))
102	  };
103	}

1	/**
2	 * Phase Configuration Types
3	 *
4	 * Types for advanced video generation configuration.
5	 * Used across multiple tools: travel-between-images, join-clips, edit-video.

11	// =============================================================================
12	// PHASE CONFIG TYPES

14	
15	/**
16	 * LoRA configuration for a single phase
17	 */
18	export interface PhaseLoraConfig {

20	  multiplier: string; // Can be a single value "1.0" or comma-separated ramp "0.1,0.3,0.5"
21	}

32	/**

127	// =============================================================================
128	// PHASE CONFIG UTILITIES
129	// =============================================================================
130	
131	/**
132	 * User LoRA input format for buildBasicModePhaseConfig
133	 */
134	interface UserLoraInput {
135	  path: string;
136	  strength: number;
137	  /** For multi-stage LoRAs - low noise variant path */
138	  lowNoisePath?: string;
139	  /** Whether this is a multi-stage LoRA (high/low noise variants) */
140	  isMultiStage?: boolean;
141	}
142	
143	/**
144	 * Build a default phase config with user LoRAs for basic mode.
145	 * This is the single source of truth for basic mode phase config generation.
146	 * Used by both batch generation (generateVideoService.ts) and individual segment
147	 * regeneration (individualTravelSegment.ts).
148	 *
149	 * @param useVaceModel - Whether to use VACE model (determines base config)
150	 * @param _amountOfMotion - 0-1 motion value (NOT 0-100)
151	 * @param userLoras - User-selected LoRAs to add to each phase
152	 * @returns PhaseConfig with base LoRAs + motion LoRA + user LoRAs
153	 */
154	export function buildBasicModePhaseConfig(
155	  useVaceModel: boolean,
156	  _amountOfMotion: number,
157	  userLoras: UserLoraInput[]
158	): PhaseConfig {
159	  const baseConfig = useVaceModel ? DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG : DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG;
160	  const totalPhases = baseConfig.phases.length;
161	
162	  return {
163	    ...baseConfig,
164	    steps_per_phase: [...baseConfig.steps_per_phase],
165	    phases: baseConfig.phases.map((phase, phaseIndex) => {
166	      const isLastPhase = phaseIndex === totalPhases - 1;
167	      const additionalLoras: PhaseLoraConfig[] = [];
168	
169	      // Add user-selected LoRAs with multi-stage support
170	      userLoras.forEach(lora => {
171	        if (lora.isMultiStage) {
172	          // Multi-stage LoRA: route to correct phase based on noise level
173	          if (isLastPhase) {
174	            // Final phase: use low_noise LoRA if available
175	            if (lora.lowNoisePath) {
176	              additionalLoras.push({
177	                url: lora.lowNoisePath,
178	                multiplier: lora.strength.toFixed(2)
179	              });
180	            }
181	          } else {
182	            // Early phases: use high_noise LoRA (main path)
183	            if (lora.path) {
184	              additionalLoras.push({
185	                url: lora.path,
186	                multiplier: lora.strength.toFixed(2)
187	              });
188	            }
189	          }
190	        } else {
191	          // Single-stage LoRA: apply to all phases
192	          if (lora.path) {
193	            additionalLoras.push({
194	              url: lora.path,
195	              multiplier: lora.strength.toFixed(2)
196	            });
197	          }
198	        }
199	      });
200	
201	      return {
202	        ...phase,
203	        loras: [
204	          ...phase.loras.map(l => ({ ...l })), // Deep clone base LoRAs
205	          ...additionalLoras
206	        ]
207	      };
208	    })
209	  };
210	}
211

56	interface DefaultableFieldResult {
57	  isUsingDefault: boolean;
58	  displayValue: string;
59	}
60	
61	export function getDefaultableField(
62	  localValue: string | undefined,
63	  defaultValue: string | undefined,
64	  hasDbOverride?: boolean
65	): DefaultableFieldResult {
66	  const isUsingDefault = localValue === undefined && (
67	    hasDbOverride !== undefined
68	      ? !hasDbOverride && defaultValue !== undefined
69	      : defaultValue !== undefined
70	  );
71	
72	  return {
73	    isUsingDefault,
74	    displayValue: isUsingDefault ? (defaultValue ?? '') : (localValue ?? ''),
75	  };
76	}
77	
78	export function detectGenerationMode(modelName?: string): 'i2v' | 'vace' {
79	  if (!modelName) return 'i2v';
80	  return modelName.toLowerCase().includes('vace') ? 'vace' : 'i2v';
81	}
82	
83	
84	export interface ShotBatchSettings {
85	  amountOfMotion?: number;
86	  motionMode?: 'basic' | 'advanced';
87	  selectedLoras?: LoraConfig[];
88	  phaseConfig?: PhaseConfig;
89	  prompt?: string;
90	  negativePrompt?: string;
91	  [key: string]: unknown;
92	}
93	
94	type PhaseConfigRequest = Omit<PhaseConfig, 'mode'>;
95	
96	// Strip mode field from phase config (backend determines mode from model)
97	export function stripModeFromPhaseConfig(config: PhaseConfig): PhaseConfigRequest {
98	  const { mode: _mode, ...rest } = config;
99	  return rest;
100	}
101	
102	interface MotionTaskFieldsInput {
103	  amountOfMotion: number;
104	  motionMode: string;
105	  phaseConfig?: PhaseConfig;
106	  selectedPhasePresetId?: string | null;
107	  omitBasicPhaseConfig?: boolean;
108	}
109	
110	export function buildMotionTaskFields({
111	  amountOfMotion,
112	  motionMode,
113	  phaseConfig,
114	  selectedPhasePresetId,
115	  omitBasicPhaseConfig = false,
116	}: MotionTaskFieldsInput): Record<string, unknown> {
117	  const shouldIncludePhaseConfig = !omitBasicPhaseConfig || motionMode !== 'basic' || !!selectedPhasePresetId;
118	
119	  if (!phaseConfig) {
120	    return {};
121	  }
122	
123	  return {
124	    amount_of_motion: amountOfMotion / 100,
125	    motion_mode: motionMode,
126	    phase_config: shouldIncludePhaseConfig ? stripModeFromPhaseConfig(phaseConfig) : undefined,
127	    selected_phase_preset_id: selectedPhasePresetId || undefined,
128	  };
129	}
130	
131	export function buildTaskParams(
132	  settings: SegmentSettings,
133	  context: {
134	    projectId: string;
135	    shotId?: string;
136	    generationId?: string;
137	    childGenerationId?: string;
138	    segmentIndex: number;
139	    startImageUrl: string;
140	    endImageUrl?: string; // Optional for trailing segments (single-image-to-video)
141	    startImageGenerationId?: string;
142	    endImageGenerationId?: string;
143	    pairShotGenerationId?: string;
144	    startImageVariantId?: string;
145	    endImageVariantId?: string;
146	    projectResolution?: string;
147	    modelName?: string;
148	    modelType?: 'i2v' | 'vace';
149	    continuationConfig?: ContinuationConfig;
150	    frameOverlapFromPrevious?: number;
151	    // Optional enhanced prompt (AI-enhanced version, kept separate from base_prompt)
152	    enhancedPrompt?: string;
153	    // Canonical travel guidance input for this segment.
154	    travelGuidance?: TravelGuidance;
155	    // When true, skip phase_config and motion fields entirely (model doesn't support them)
156	    skipMotionFields?: boolean;
157	    // Original task params from the generation being retried (preserves pipeline layout)
158	    originalParams?: Record<string, unknown>;
159	  }
160	): IndividualTravelSegmentParams {
161	  const motionFields = context.skipMotionFields
162	    ? {}
163	    : buildMotionTaskFields({
164	      amountOfMotion: settings.amountOfMotion,
165	      motionMode: settings.motionMode,
166	      phaseConfig: settings.phaseConfig ?? DEFAULT_PHASE_CONFIG,
167	      selectedPhasePresetId: settings.selectedPhasePresetId,
168	      omitBasicPhaseConfig: true,
169	    });
170	
171	  // Detect trailing segment: no end image means single-image-to-video (last segment)
172	  const isTrailingSegment = !context.endImageUrl;
173	
174	  return {
175	    project_id: context.projectId,
176	    shot_id: context.shotId,
177	    parent_generation_id: context.generationId,
178	    child_generation_id: context.childGenerationId,
179	    segment_index: context.segmentIndex,
180	    start_image_url: context.startImageUrl,
181	    end_image_url: context.endImageUrl,
182	    // For trailing segments (no end image), mark as last segment so worker uses I2V mode
183	    is_last_segment: isTrailingSegment,
184	    start_image_generation_id: context.startImageGenerationId,
185	    end_image_generation_id: context.endImageGenerationId,
186	    pair_shot_generation_id: context.pairShotGenerationId,
187	    start_image_variant_id: context.startImageVariantId,
188	    end_image_variant_id: context.endImageVariantId,
189	    // Settings
190	    base_prompt: settings.prompt,
191	    negative_prompt: settings.negativePrompt,
192	    // Enhanced prompt (AI-enhanced version) - worker should prefer this over base_prompt when present
193	    ...(context.enhancedPrompt && { enhanced_prompt: context.enhancedPrompt }),
194	    num_frames: settings.numFrames,
195	    random_seed: settings.randomSeed,
196	    seed: settings.seed,
197	    ...motionFields,
198	    loras: settings.loras.map(l => ({ path: l.path, strength: l.strength })),
199	    ...(settings.inferenceSteps !== undefined && { num_inference_steps: settings.inferenceSteps }),
200	    ...(settings.guidanceScale !== undefined && { guidance_scale: settings.guidanceScale }),
201	    make_primary_variant: settings.makePrimaryVariant,
202	    // Resolution
203	    ...(context.projectResolution && { parsed_resolution_wh: context.projectResolution }),
204	    ...(context.modelName && { model_name: context.modelName }),
205	    ...(context.modelType && { model_type: context.modelType }),
206	    ...(context.continuationConfig && { continuation_config: context.continuationConfig }),
207	    ...(context.frameOverlapFromPrevious !== undefined ? { frame_overlap_from_previous: context.frameOverlapFromPrevious } : {}),
208	    ...(context.travelGuidance && { travel_guidance: context.travelGuidance }),
209	    ...(context.originalParams && { originalParams: context.originalParams }),
210	  };
211	}
212

17	    throw new ValidationError(
18	      'Travel payload integrity check failed: image_generation_ids count does not match image_urls count.',

39	    actualModelName,
40	    selectedModel,
41	    policy,

47	  const {

53	    numInferenceSteps,

65	    seed,
66	    randomSeed,
67	    turboMode,
68	    debug,
69	  } = seedParams;
70	  const imageCount = imagePayload.absoluteImageUrls.length;
71	  const expectedPairCount = Math.max(0, imageCount - 1);
72	  assertMappedIdCardinality(imagePayload, imageCount);
73	  const spec = getModelSpec(selectedModel ?? resolveSelectedModelFromModelName(actualModelName));
74	  const effectivePolicy = policy ?? resolveGenerationPolicy(spec, {
75	    smoothContinuations: false,
76	    requestedExecutionMode: generationTypeMode ?? 'i2v',
77	  });
78	
79	  const motionFields = spec.supportsMotionFields
80	    ? buildMotionTaskFields({
81	      amountOfMotion,
82	      motionMode,
83	      phaseConfig: effectivePhaseConfig,
84	      selectedPhasePresetId,
85	      omitBasicPhaseConfig: true,
86	    })
87	    : {};
88	
89	  const hasValidEnhancedPrompts = pairConfig.enhancedPromptsArray.some(prompt => prompt && prompt.trim().length > 0);
90	  const pairPhaseConfigsArray = spec.supportsPhaseConfig
91	    ? pairConfig.pairPhaseConfigsArray
92	    : pairConfig.pairPhaseConfigsArray.map(() => null);
93	
94	  const continuationConfig = effectivePolicy.continuation.enabled && effectivePolicy.continuation.strategy
95	    ? {
96	      strategy: effectivePolicy.continuation.strategy,
97	      overlap_frames: effectivePolicy.continuation.overlapFrames,
98	    }
99	    : undefined;
100	
101	  return {

107	      : {}),

110	      : {}),
111	    ...(parentGenerationId ? { parent_generation_id: parentGenerationId } : {}),
112	    base_prompts: pairConfig.basePrompts,

114	    segment_frames: pairConfig.segmentFrames,
115	    frame_overlap: pairConfig.frameOverlap,
116	    ...(continuationConfig ? { continuation_config: continuationConfig } : {}),
117	    negative_prompts: pairConfig.negativePrompts,
118	    ...(hasValidEnhancedPrompts ? { enhanced_prompts: pairConfig.enhancedPromptsArray } : {}),
119	    ...(pairPhaseConfigsArray.some(config => config !== null) ? { pair_phase_configs: pairPhaseConfigsArray } : {}),
120	    ...(pairConfig.pairLorasArray.some(loras => loras !== null) ? { pair_loras: pairConfig.pairLorasArray } : {}),
121	    ...(pairConfig.pairMotionSettingsArray.some(settings => settings !== null) ? { pair_motion_settings: pairConfig.pairMotionSettingsArray } : {}),
122	    model_name: actualModelName,
123	    model_type: effectivePolicy.travelMode,
124	    seed,
125	    debug: debug ?? DEFAULT_STEERABLE_MOTION_SETTINGS.debug,
126	    show_input_images: DEFAULT_STEERABLE_MOTION_SETTINGS.show_input_images,
127	    ...(!hasValidEnhancedPrompts ? { enhance_prompt: enhancePrompt } : {}),
128	    generation_mode: generationMode,
129	    random_seed: randomSeed,
130	    turbo_mode: turboMode,
131	    ...(spec.ui.inferenceSteps && numInferenceSteps !== undefined ? { num_inference_steps: numInferenceSteps } : {}),
132	    ...(spec.ui.guidanceScale && guidanceScale !== undefined ? { guidance_scale: guidanceScale } : {}),
133	    ...motionFields,

135	    generation_name: variantNameParam.trim() || undefined,

137	    ...(textAfterPrompts ? { text_after_prompts: textAfterPrompts } : {}),
138	    independent_segments: !effectivePolicy.continuation.enabled,
139	    chain_segments: effectivePolicy.continuation.enabled,

1	import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react';
2	import type { PhaseConfig } from '../../settings';
3	import type { PresetMetadata } from '@/shared/types/presetMetadata';
4	
5	interface UsePresetAutoSelectParams {
6	  /** Current generation type mode (I2V vs VACE) */
7	  generationTypeMode: 'i2v' | 'vace';
8	  /** Whether a structure video is currently set */
9	  hasStructureVideo: boolean;
10	  /** The built-in default preset for the current mode */
11	  builtinDefaultPreset: {
12	    id: string;
13	    metadata: { name: string; description: string; phaseConfig: PhaseConfig };
14	  };
15	  /** Currently selected preset ID */
16	  selectedPhasePresetId?: string | null;
17	  /** Handler to select a preset */
18	  onPhasePresetSelect: (presetId: string, config: PhaseConfig, presetMetadata?: PresetMetadata) => void;
19	  /** Whether settings are still loading */
20	  settingsLoading?: boolean;
21	  /** Current motion mode (basic or advanced) */
22	  motionMode: 'basic' | 'advanced';
23	  /** Current phase config */
24	  phaseConfig?: PhaseConfig;
25	}
26	
27	/**
28	 * Auto-selects the appropriate built-in default preset when:
29	 * 1. Initial mount with no preset selected AND user is in basic mode
30	 * 2. Generation type mode changes (I2V vs VACE) - only in basic mode
31	 *
32	 * Tracks generationTypeMode instead of hasStructureVideo because
33	 * Uni3C has structure video but uses I2V.
34	 */
35	export function usePresetAutoSelect({
36	  generationTypeMode,
37	  hasStructureVideo,
38	  builtinDefaultPreset,
39	  selectedPhasePresetId,
40	  onPhasePresetSelect,
41	  settingsLoading,
42	  motionMode,
43	  phaseConfig,
44	}: UsePresetAutoSelectParams) {
45	  // Track previous state for auto-switching presets
46	  const prevHasStructureVideoRef = useRef<boolean | undefined>(undefined);
47	  const prevGenerationTypeModeRef = useRef<'i2v' | 'vace' | undefined>(undefined);
48	
49	  // Track if we've done initial auto-select
50	  const hasAutoSelectedRef = useRef(false);
51	
52	  // Reset hasAutoSelectedRef when settings start loading (new shot navigation)
53	  // This ensures auto-select can run for each shot, not just the first one visited
54	  useEffect(() => {
55	    if (settingsLoading) {
56	      hasAutoSelectedRef.current = false;
57	    }
58	  }, [settingsLoading]);
59	
60	  // Auto-select the built-in default preset when:
61	  // 1. Initial mount with no preset selected AND user is in basic mode
62	  // 2. Generation type mode changes (I2V vs VACE) - only in basic mode
63	  // Note: We track generationTypeMode instead of hasStructureVideo because Uni3C has structure video but uses I2V
64	  useEffect(() => {
65	    // Skip if settings are still loading
66	    if (settingsLoading) {
67	      return;
68	    }
69	
70	    // CRITICAL FIX: Skip auto-select when user is in advanced mode
71	    // In advanced mode, user has explicitly chosen to configure phaseConfig manually
72	    // Auto-selecting a preset would overwrite their custom configuration
73	    if (motionMode === 'advanced') {
74	      prevHasStructureVideoRef.current = hasStructureVideo;
75	      prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current = generationTypeMode;
76	      return;
77	    }
78	
79	    // Also skip if phaseConfig already exists with valid data
80	    // This prevents overwriting user's config when remounting
81	    if (phaseConfig && phaseConfig.phases && phaseConfig.phases.length > 0) {
82	      // Only auto-select if no preset is selected (user may have deselected preset but kept config)
83	      // But still allow generation type mode change to trigger preset switch
84	      const modeChanged =
85	        prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current !== undefined &&
86	        prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current !== generationTypeMode;
87	
88	      if (!modeChanged) {
89	        prevHasStructureVideoRef.current = hasStructureVideo;
90	        prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current = generationTypeMode;
91	        return;
92	      }
93	    }
94	
95	    const modeChanged =
96	      prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current !== undefined &&
97	      prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current !== generationTypeMode;
98	
99	    // When generation type mode changes, select appropriate default (only in basic mode)
100	    // This handles switching between I2V and VACE (including Uni3C which uses I2V with structure video)
101	    if (modeChanged) {
102	      onPhasePresetSelect(
103	        builtinDefaultPreset.id,
104	        builtinDefaultPreset.metadata.phaseConfig,
105	        builtinDefaultPreset.metadata
106	      );
107	      prevHasStructureVideoRef.current = hasStructureVideo;
108	      prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current = generationTypeMode;
109	      return;
110	    }
111	
112	    // Initial auto-select: only if no preset selected and we haven't auto-selected yet
113	    if (!selectedPhasePresetId && !hasAutoSelectedRef.current) {
114	      hasAutoSelectedRef.current = true;
115	      onPhasePresetSelect(
116	        builtinDefaultPreset.id,
117	        builtinDefaultPreset.metadata.phaseConfig,
118	        builtinDefaultPreset.metadata
119	      );
120	    }
121	
122	    prevHasStructureVideoRef.current = hasStructureVideo;
123	    prevGenerationTypeModeRef.current = generationTypeMode;
124	  }, [
125	    generationTypeMode,
126	    hasStructureVideo,
127	    builtinDefaultPreset,
128	    selectedPhasePresetId,
129	    onPhasePresetSelect,
130	    settingsLoading,
131	    motionMode,
132	    phaseConfig,
133	  ]);
134	}
135

1	import { useCallback } from 'react';
2	import { coerceSelectedModel, getModelSpec, MODEL_DEFAULTS } from '@/tools/travel-between-images/settings';
3	import type { SelectedModel } from '@/tools/travel-between-images/settings';
4	import type { SegmentSettings, SegmentSettingsFormProps } from '../types';
5	
6	/**
7	 * Handles model switching with proper cleanup of model-dependent settings.
8	 * Shared between PromptSection (model toggle) and AdvancedSettingsSection (model pills).
9	 */
10	export function useModelChange({
11	  onChange,
12	  settings,
13	  shotDefaults,
14	}: {
15	  onChange: (updates: Partial<SegmentSettings>) => void;
16	  settings: SegmentSettings;
17	  shotDefaults?: SegmentSettingsFormProps['shotDefaults'];
18	}) {
19	  const effectiveSelectedModel = coerceSelectedModel(
20	    settings.selectedModel ?? shotDefaults?.selectedModel,
21	  );
22	
23	  const handleModelChange = useCallback(
24	    (nextModel: SelectedModel) => {
25	      const currentSpec = getModelSpec(effectiveSelectedModel);
26	      const nextSpec = getModelSpec(nextModel);
27	      const nextDefaults = MODEL_DEFAULTS[nextModel];
28	
29	      const updates: Partial<SegmentSettings> = {
30	        selectedModel: nextModel,
31	        inferenceSteps: nextDefaults.steps,
32	        guidanceScale: nextDefaults.guidanceScale,
33	      };
34	
35	      // Clear LoRAs when switching between model families (incompatible weights)
36	      if (currentSpec.loraFamily !== nextSpec.loraFamily) {
37	        updates.loras = [];
38	      }
39	
40	      // Clear phase config when switching to a model that doesn't support it
41	      if (currentSpec.supportsPhaseConfig && !nextSpec.supportsPhaseConfig) {
42	        updates.phaseConfig = undefined;
43	        updates.selectedPhasePresetId = null;
44	        updates.motionMode = 'basic';
45	      }
46	
47	      onChange(updates);
48	    },
49	    [effectiveSelectedModel, onChange],
50	  );
51	
52	  return handleModelChange;
53	}
54

17	/**
18	 * Shared component for Basic/Advanced motion settings with preset selection.
19	 * 
20	 * Features:
21	 * - Basic mode: Preset chips for quick selection + optional custom content (e.g., LoRAs)
22	 * - Advanced mode: Full PhaseConfigVertical editor
23	 * - Browse modal for discovering more presets
24	 * - Persists selections via callbacks
25	 * 
26	 * @example
27	 * ```tsx
28	 * <MotionPresetSelector
29	 *   builtinPreset={BUILTIN_JOIN_CLIPS_PRESET}
30	 *   generationTypeMode="vace"
31	 *   selectedPhasePresetId={selectedPhasePresetId}
32	 *   phaseConfig={phaseConfig}
33	 *   motionMode={motionMode}
34	 *   onPresetSelect={handlePresetSelect}
35	 *   onPresetRemove={handlePresetRemove}
36	 *   onModeChange={handleModeChange}
37	 *   onPhaseConfigChange={handlePhaseConfigChange}
38	 *   renderBasicModeContent={() => (
39	 *     <LoraManager ... />
40	 *   )}
41	 * />
42	 * ```
43	 */
44	export const MotionPresetSelector: React.FC<MotionPresetSelectorProps> = ({
45	  // Core config
46	  builtinPreset,
47	  featuredPresetIds = [],
48	  generationTypeMode,
49	  
50	  // State
51	  selectedPhasePresetId,
52	  phaseConfig,
53	  motionMode,
54	  
55	  // Callbacks
56	  onPresetSelect,
57	  onPresetRemove,
58	  onModeChange,
59	  onPhaseConfigChange,
60	  onRestoreDefaults,
61	  
62	  // Advanced mode props
63	  availableLoras = [],
64	  randomSeed = true,
65	  onRandomSeedChange,
66	  
67	  // Optional behavior
68	  advancedDisabled = false,
69	  advancedDisabledReason,
70	  presetTooltip = 'Select a motion preset to control generation settings.\nUse "Custom" to configure your own settings in Advanced mode.',
71	  renderBasicModeContent,
72	  queryKeyPrefix = 'motion-presets',
73	  labelSuffix,
74	}) => {
75	  // Defensive: some callsites may pass non-functions (e.g. via `any`).
76	  // Avoid crashing the entire UI; treat invalid callbacks as no-ops.
77	  const safeOnModeChange = useCallback((mode: MotionMode) => {
78	    if (typeof onModeChange === 'function') {
79	      onModeChange(mode);
80	    }
81	  }, [onModeChange]);
82	  const safeOnPresetRemove = useCallback(() => {
83	    if (typeof onPresetRemove === 'function') {
84	      onPresetRemove();
85	    }
86	  }, [onPresetRemove]);
87	  const safeOnPresetSelect = useCallback((id: string, config: PhaseConfig, metadata?: PresetMetadata) => {
88	    if (typeof onPresetSelect === 'function') {
89	      onPresetSelect(id, config, metadata);
90	    }
91	  }, [onPresetSelect]);
92	  const safeOnPhaseConfigChange = useCallback((config: PhaseConfig) => {
93	    if (typeof onPhaseConfigChange === 'function') {
94	      onPhaseConfigChange(config);
95	    }
96	  }, [onPhaseConfigChange]);
97	
98	  // Normalize possibly-missing/invalid mode values coming from persisted settings.
99	  // This prevents the Tabs from ending up with "no selection".
100	  const normalizedMotionMode: MotionMode =
101	    advancedDisabled ? 'basic' : (motionMode === 'advanced' ? 'advanced' : 'basic');
102	
103	  // Preset modal state
104	  const [isPresetModalOpen, setIsPresetModalOpen] = useState(false);
105	
106	  // Use shared preset hook
107	  const {
108	    allPresets,
109	    shouldShowPresetChips,
110	    isCustomConfig,
111	  } = useMotionPresets({
112	    builtinPreset,
113	    featuredPresetIds,
114	    selectedPhasePresetId,
115	    queryKeyPrefix,
116	  });
117	
118	  // Handle motion mode change
119	  const handleModeChange = useCallback((newMode: string) => {
120	    if (newMode === normalizedMotionMode) return;
121	    if (advancedDisabled && newMode === 'advanced') return;
122	    safeOnModeChange(newMode as MotionMode);
123	  }, [normalizedMotionMode, advancedDisabled, safeOnModeChange]);
124	
125	  // Handle editing a preset (load its config and switch to advanced)
126	  const handleEditPreset = useCallback((presetPhaseConfig: PhaseConfig | undefined) => {
127	    if (presetPhaseConfig) {
128	      safeOnPhaseConfigChange(presetPhaseConfig);
129	    }
130	    safeOnPresetRemove();
131	    safeOnModeChange('advanced');
132	  }, [safeOnPhaseConfigChange, safeOnPresetRemove, safeOnModeChange]);
133	
134	  // Handle preset selection (from chips or modal)
135	  const handlePresetSelect = useCallback((preset: Preset) => {
136	    if (preset.metadata?.phaseConfig) {
137	      safeOnPresetSelect(preset.id, preset.metadata.phaseConfig, preset.metadata);
138	    }
139	    setIsPresetModalOpen(false);
140	  }, [safeOnPresetSelect]);
141	
142	  // Adapter for modal's onSelectPreset (it passes a Resource type)
143	  const handleModalPresetSelect = useCallback((resource: { id: string; metadata: PresetMetadata }) => {
144	    handlePresetSelect(resource as Preset);
145	  }, [handlePresetSelect]);
146	
147	  // Handle switching to custom/advanced mode
148	  const handleCustomClick = useCallback(() => {
149	    safeOnPresetRemove();
150	    safeOnModeChange('advanced');
151	  }, [safeOnPresetRemove, safeOnModeChange]);
152	
153	  // Handle restore defaults for phase config
154	  const handleRestorePhaseConfigDefaults = useCallback(() => {
155	    safeOnPhaseConfigChange(builtinPreset.metadata.phaseConfig);
156	    safeOnPresetSelect(builtinPreset.id, builtinPreset.metadata.phaseConfig, builtinPreset.metadata);
157	  }, [builtinPreset, safeOnPhaseConfigChange, safeOnPresetSelect]);
158	
159	  return (
160	    <div className="space-y-4">
161	      <Tabs value={normalizedMotionMode} onValueChange={handleModeChange}>
162	        <div className="flex items-center gap-3 mb-3">
163	          <Label className="text-sm font-medium">Mode:</Label>
164	          {labelSuffix}
165	          <TabsList className="grid w-40 grid-cols-2">
166	            <TabsTrigger value="basic">Basic</TabsTrigger>
167	            <TabsTrigger
168	              value="advanced"
169	              disabled={advancedDisabled}
170	              title={advancedDisabled ? advancedDisabledReason : undefined}
171	            >
172	              Advanced
173	            </TabsTrigger>
174	          </TabsList>
175	        </div>
176	
177	        {/* Basic Mode: Preset Selector + Optional Custom Content */}
178	        <TabsContent value="basic" className="mt-0 space-y-4">
179	          {/* Preset Selection Section */}
180	          <div className="space-y-3">
181	            <MotionPresetSectionHeader
182	              tooltipContent={presetTooltip}
183	              tooltipContentClassName="whitespace-pre-line"
184	              onBrowsePresets={() => setIsPresetModalOpen(true)}
185	            />
186	
187	            {/* Preset chips - show if no selection or selection is a known preset */}
188	            {shouldShowPresetChips ? (
189	              <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 items-stretch">
190	                {/* Built-in + additional presets */}

193	                  const isBuiltinDefault = preset.id === builtinPreset.id;
194	                  const metadata = preset.metadata;

196	                  
197	                  return (

199	                      <button
200	                        onClick={() => handlePresetSelect(preset)}
201	                        className={`

203	                          ${isSelected 
204	                            ? 'bg-primary/20 border-primary text-primary ring-2 ring-primary/30' 
205	                            : isBuiltinDefault
206	                              ? 'bg-muted border-primary/30 hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-muted/80'
207	                              : 'bg-muted/50 border-border hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-muted'
208	                          }
209	                        `}
210	                      >

212	                        {sampleVideo && (

215	                              src={sampleVideo.url}
216	                              className="w-full h-full object-cover"
217	                            />
218	                          </div>
219	                        )}
220	                        <div className="flex min-w-0 flex-col justify-center items-start text-left flex-1">
221	                          {isBuiltinDefault ? (
222	                            <span className="text-sm font-medium leading-tight preserve-case">
223	                              {metadata?.name || 'Preset'}{' '}
224	                              <span className="font-normal text-muted-foreground">(default)</span>
225	                            </span>
226	                          ) : (

228	                              {metadata?.name || 'Preset'}
229	                            </span>
230	                          )}
231	                        </div>
232	                      </button>
233	                      {/* Edit button - appears on hover */}
234	                      <button
235	                        onClick={(e) => {

238	                        }}
239	                        className="absolute top-1 right-1 p-1 rounded bg-muted/80 border border-border opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 hover:bg-primary hover:text-primary-foreground transition-all"
240	                        title="Edit in Advanced mode"
241	                      >
242	                        <Pencil className="h-3 w-3" />
243	                      </button>
244	                    </div>
245	                  );
246	                })}
247	
248	                {/* Custom chip - explicitly goes to advanced mode */}
249	                <button
250	                  onClick={handleCustomClick}
251	                  className={`
252	                    relative group flex items-center justify-center gap-2 px-3 py-2 rounded-lg border transition-colors
253	                    ${isCustomConfig
254	                      ? 'bg-primary/20 border-primary text-primary ring-2 ring-primary/30' 
255	                      : 'bg-muted/50 border-border hover:border-primary/50 hover:bg-muted'
256	                    }
257	                  `}
258	                >

263	            ) : (

271	              />
272	            )}
273	          </div>
274	
275	          {/* Optional custom content for Basic mode (e.g., LoRA manager) */}
276	          {renderBasicModeContent && (
277	            <div className="pt-4 border-t">
278	              {renderBasicModeContent()}
279	            </div>
280	          )}
281	        </TabsContent>
282	
283	        {/* Advanced Mode: Phase Configuration */}
284	        <TabsContent value="advanced" className="mt-0">
285	          {phaseConfig ? (
286	            <PhaseConfigVertical
287	              phaseConfig={phaseConfig}
288	              onPhaseConfigChange={onPhaseConfigChange}
289	              randomSeed={randomSeed}
290	              onRandomSeedChange={onRandomSeedChange || (() => {})}
291	              availableLoras={availableLoras}
292	              generationTypeMode={generationTypeMode}
293	              onRestoreDefaults={onRestoreDefaults || handleRestorePhaseConfigDefaults}
294	              selectedPhasePresetId={selectedPhasePresetId}
295	              onPhasePresetSelect={(presetId, config, presetMetadata) => {
296	                onPresetSelect(presetId, config, presetMetadata as PresetMetadata | undefined);
297	              }}
298	              onPhasePresetRemove={onPresetRemove}
299	            />
300	          ) : (
301	            <div className="text-sm text-muted-foreground p-4 border rounded-lg bg-muted/30">
302	              No phase configuration available. Switch to Basic mode to select a preset.
303	            </div>
304	          )}
305	        </TabsContent>
306	      </Tabs>
307	
308	      {/* Phase Config Selector Modal */}
309	      <PhaseConfigSelectorModal
310	        isOpen={isPresetModalOpen}
311	        onClose={() => setIsPresetModalOpen(false)}
312	        onSelectPreset={handleModalPresetSelect}
313	        onRemovePreset={onPresetRemove}
314	        selectedPresetId={selectedPhasePresetId}
315	        currentPhaseConfig={phaseConfig}
316	        generationTypeMode={generationTypeMode}
317	      />
318	    </div>
319	  );
320	};
321	
322	// Note: SelectedPresetCard and useMotionPresets are internal - not exported
323

7	/**

11	export function buildBasicModeGenerationRequest(

14	): { model: string; phaseConfig: PhaseConfig } {

17	  return { model, phaseConfig };

20	export function resolveModelPhaseSelection(
21	  amountOfMotion: number,
22	  advancedMode: boolean,
23	  phaseConfig: PhaseConfig | undefined,
24	  motionMode: MotionConfig['motion_mode'],
25	  selectedLoras: GenerateVideoParams['selectedLoras'],
26	): ModelPhaseSelection {
27	  const userLorasForPhaseConfig = (selectedLoras || []).map((lora) => ({
28	    path: lora.path,
29	    strength: parseFloat(lora.strength?.toString() ?? '0') || 0.0,
30	    lowNoisePath: (lora as { lowNoisePath?: string }).lowNoisePath,
31	    isMultiStage: (lora as { isMultiStage?: boolean }).isMultiStage,
32	  }));
33	
34	  const useAdvancedMode = Boolean(advancedMode && phaseConfig && motionMode !== 'basic');
35	  if (useAdvancedMode && phaseConfig) {
36	    return {
37	      actualModelName: phaseConfig.num_phases === 2
38	        ? 'wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_3_3'
39	        : 'wan_2_2_i2v_lightning_baseline_2_2_2',
40	      effectivePhaseConfig: phaseConfig,
41	      useAdvancedMode,
42	    };
43	  }
44	
45	  const basicConfig = buildBasicModeGenerationRequest(amountOfMotion, userLorasForPhaseConfig);
46	  return {
47	    actualModelName: basicConfig.model,
48	    effectivePhaseConfig: basicConfig.phaseConfig,
49	    useAdvancedMode: false,
50	  };
51	}
52	
53	export function validatePhaseConfigConsistency(config: PhaseConfig): void {
54	  const phasesLength = config.phases?.length || 0;
55	  const stepsLength = config.steps_per_phase?.length || 0;
56	  if (config.num_phases !== phasesLength || config.num_phases !== stepsLength) {
57	    throw new ValidationError(
58	      `Invalid phase configuration: num_phases (${config.num_phases}) does not match arrays (phases: ${phasesLength}, steps: ${stepsLength}).`,
59	    );
60	  }
61	}
62	
63	export function resolveLtxModelSelection(selectedModel: SelectedModel): ModelPhaseSelection {
64	  return {
65	    actualModelName: (MODEL_DEFAULTS[selectedModel] ?? MODEL_DEFAULTS['wan-2.2']).modelName,
66	    effectivePhaseConfig: undefined,
67	    useAdvancedMode: false,
68	  };
69	}

15	export const applyModelSettings = (
16	  settings: ExtractedGenerationSettings,
17	  context: ApplyModelContext,
18	): ApplyResult => {
19	  if (settings.selectedModel && context.onSelectedModelChange) {
20	    context.onSelectedModelChange(settings.selectedModel);
21	  }
22	
23	  if (!settings.model || settings.model === context.steerableMotionSettings.model_name) {
24	    return { success: true, settingName: 'model', details: 'skipped - no change' };
25	  }
26	
27	  context.onSteerableMotionSettingsChange({ model_name: settings.model });
28	
29	  return { success: true, settingName: 'model', details: settings.model };
30	};
31	
32	export const applyPromptSettings = async (
33	  settings: ExtractedPromptSettings,
34	  context: ApplyPromptContext,
35	): Promise<ApplyResult> => {
36	  // Apply main prompt
37	  if (typeof settings.prompt === 'string' && settings.prompt.trim()) {
38	    context.onBatchVideoPromptChange(settings.prompt);
39	  }
40	
41	  // Apply individual prompts to pair configs (regardless of current mode)
42	  if (settings.prompts && settings.prompts.length > 1 && context.updatePairPromptsByIndex) {
43	    const errors: string[] = [];
44	
45	    for (let i = 0; i < settings.prompts.length; i++) {
46	      const pairPrompt = settings.prompts[i]?.trim();
47	      const pairNegativePrompt = settings.negativePrompts?.[i]?.trim() || '';
48	
49	      if (pairPrompt) {
50	        try {
51	          await context.updatePairPromptsByIndex(i, pairPrompt, pairNegativePrompt);
52	        } catch (e) {
53	          normalizeAndPresentError(e, {
54	            context: 'ApplySettings.applyPromptSettings',
55	            showToast: false,
56	            logData: { pairIndex: i },
57	          });
58	          errors.push(`Pair ${i}: ${e}`);
59	        }
60	      }
61	    }
62	
63	    if (errors.length > 0) {
64	      return { success: false, settingName: 'prompts', error: errors.join('; ') };
65	    }
66	  }
67	
68	  // Apply negative prompt
69	  if (settings.negativePrompt !== undefined) {
70	    context.onSteerableMotionSettingsChange({ negative_prompt: settings.negativePrompt || '' });
71	  }
72	
73	  return { success: true, settingName: 'prompts' };
74	};

76	export const applyGenerationSettings = (
77	  settings: ExtractedGenerationSettings,

80	  // Apply frames
81	  if (typeof settings.frames === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(settings.frames)) {
82	    context.onBatchVideoFramesChange(settings.frames);
83	  }
84	
85	  // Apply steps
86	  if (typeof settings.steps === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(settings.steps)) {
87	    context.onBatchVideoStepsChange(settings.steps);
88	  }
89	
90	  if (typeof settings.guidanceScale === 'number' && !Number.isNaN(settings.guidanceScale)) {
91	    context.onGuidanceScaleChange?.(settings.guidanceScale);
92	  }
93	
94	  return { success: true, settingName: 'generation' };

97	export const applyModeSettings = (
98	  settings: ExtractedModeSettings,

100	): ApplyResult => {
101	  // Apply generation mode
102	  if (settings.generationMode && (settings.generationMode === 'batch' || settings.generationMode === 'timeline' || settings.generationMode === 'by-pair')) {
103	    context.onGenerationModeChange(settings.generationMode);
104	  }
105	
106	  // Apply advanced mode
107	  if (settings.advancedMode !== undefined) {
108	    context.onAdvancedModeChange(settings.advancedMode);
109	  }
110	
111	  // Apply motion mode
112	  if (
113	    settings.motionMode !== undefined
114	    && settings.motionMode !== 'presets'
115	    && context.onMotionModeChange
116	  ) {
117	    context.onMotionModeChange(settings.motionMode);
118	  }
119	
120	  // Apply generation type mode (I2V vs VACE)
121	  if (settings.generationTypeMode !== undefined && context.onGenerationTypeModeChange) {
122	    context.onGenerationTypeModeChange(settings.generationTypeMode);
123	  }
124	
125	  if (settings.smoothContinuations !== undefined && context.onSmoothContinuationsChange) {
126	    context.onSmoothContinuationsChange(settings.smoothContinuations);
127	  }
128	
129	  return { success: true, settingName: 'modes' };

132	const deepClonePhaseConfig = (config: ExtractedAdvancedSettings['phaseConfig']) => {
133	  if (!config) return undefined;

146	  context: ApplyAdvancedContext,
147	): ApplyResult => {
148	  // Apply phase config
149	  if (settings.phaseConfig) {
150	    // Deep clone prevents shared references when applying task settings.
151	    const clonedConfig = deepClonePhaseConfig(settings.phaseConfig)!;
152	    context.onPhaseConfigChange(clonedConfig);
153	  }
154	
155	  // Apply phase preset ID
156	  if (settings.selectedPhasePresetId !== undefined) {
157	    if (settings.selectedPhasePresetId && context.onPhasePresetSelect && settings.phaseConfig) {
158	      const clonedConfig = deepClonePhaseConfig(settings.phaseConfig)!;
159	      context.onPhasePresetSelect(settings.selectedPhasePresetId, clonedConfig);
160	    } else if (!settings.selectedPhasePresetId && context.onPhasePresetRemove) {
161	      context.onPhasePresetRemove();
162	    }
163	  }
164	
165	  // Apply turbo mode

167	    context.onTurboModeChange(settings.turboMode);
168	  }
169	
170	  // Apply enhance prompt

172	    context.onEnhancePromptChange(settings.enhancePrompt);
173	  }
174	
175	  return { success: true, settingName: 'advancedMode' };

1	/**
2	 * VideoTravelSettingsProvider - Centralized settings context for Video Travel tool
3	 *
4	 * This provider owns all shot-specific settings state, making it accessible to
5	 * any child component without prop drilling. Settings are persisted via useShotSettings.
6	 *
7	 * Architecture:
8	 * - Wraps useShotSettings (state + persistence)
9	 * - Wraps useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers (all update handlers)
10	 * - Exposes focused hooks for each settings domain
11	 *
12	 * Usage:
13	 * ```tsx
14	 * // In VideoTravelToolPage
15	 * <VideoTravelSettingsProvider projectId={projectId} shotId={shotId}>
16	 *   <ShotSettingsEditor />
17	 * </VideoTravelSettingsProvider>
18	 *
19	 * // In any child component
20	 * const { prompt, setPrompt } = usePromptSettings();
21	 * const { motionMode, setMotionMode } = useMotionSettings();

23	 */

46	import type { LoraModel } from '@/domains/lora/types/lora';

48	// =============================================================================
49	// CONTEXT TYPES
50	// =============================================================================
51	
52	interface VideoTravelSettingsContextValue {
53	  // Core state
54	  settings: VideoTravelSettings;
55	  status: 'idle' | 'loading' | 'ready' | 'saving' | 'error';
56	  isDirty: boolean;
57	  isLoading: boolean;
58	
59	  // Shot info
60	  shotId: string | null;
61	  projectId: string | null;
62	
63	  // All handlers from useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers
64	  handlers: VideoTravelSettingsHandlers;
65	
66	  // Direct access to updateField/updateFields for custom updates
67	  updateField: UseShotSettingsReturn['updateField'];
68	  updateFields: UseShotSettingsReturn['updateFields'];
69	
70	  // Save operations
71	  save: () => Promise<void>;
72	  saveImmediate: () => Promise<void>;
73	
74	  // LoRAs (passed through from parent)
75	  availableLoras: LoraModel[];
76	}
77	
78	// Export the context for direct useContext access in bridge hooks
79	export const VideoTravelSettingsContext = createContext<VideoTravelSettingsContextValue | null>(null);
80	
81	// =============================================================================
82	// PROVIDER COMPONENT
83	// =============================================================================
84	
85	interface VideoTravelSettingsProviderProps {
86	  projectId: string | null | undefined;
87	  shotId: string | null | undefined;
88	  selectedShot: Shot | null;
89	  availableLoras: LoraModel[];
90	  /** Function to optimistically update generation mode cache (from useProjectGenerationModesCache) */
91	  updateShotMode: (shotId: string, mode: 'batch' | 'timeline' | 'by-pair') => void;
92	  children: React.ReactNode;
93	}
94	
95	export const VideoTravelSettingsProvider: React.FC<VideoTravelSettingsProviderProps> = ({
96	  projectId,
97	  shotId,
98	  selectedShot,
99	  availableLoras,
100	  updateShotMode,
101	  children,
102	}) => {
103	  // Core settings hook - manages state + persistence
104	  const shotSettings = useShotSettings(shotId, projectId);
105	
106	  // Create ref for handlers (they need ref to avoid recreation)
107	  const shotSettingsRef = useRef(shotSettings);
108	  shotSettingsRef.current = shotSettings;
109	
110	  // All handlers
111	  const handlers = useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers({
112	    shotSettingsRef,
113	    currentShotId: shotId || null,
114	    selectedShot,
115	    updateShotMode,
116	  });
117	
118	  const setSelectedModel = useCallback((nextModel: SelectedModel) => {
119	    const currentSettings = shotSettingsRef.current.settings;
120	    const currentModel = coerceSelectedModel(currentSettings.selectedModel);
121	
122	    if (currentModel === nextModel) {
123	      return;
124	    }
125	
126	    const currentDefaults = MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel];
127	    const nextDefaults = MODEL_DEFAULTS[nextModel];
128	    const nextSpec = getModelSpec(nextModel);
129	    const currentFrames = clampFrameCountToPolicy(
130	      currentSettings.batchVideoFrames ?? currentDefaults.frames,
131	      getModelSpec(currentModel),
132	      {
133	        smoothContinuations: currentSettings.smoothContinuations ?? false,
134	        requestedExecutionMode: currentSettings.generationTypeMode ?? 'i2v',
135	      },
136	    );
137	    const modelSettingsByModel = {
138	      ...(currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel ?? {}),
139	      [currentModel]: {
140	        batchVideoFrames: currentFrames,
141	        batchVideoSteps: currentSettings.batchVideoSteps ?? currentDefaults.steps,
142	        guidanceScale: currentSettings.guidanceScale ?? currentDefaults.guidanceScale,
143	      },
144	    };
145	    const nextSubstate = modelSettingsByModel[nextModel];
146	    const nextFrames = clampFrameCountToPolicy(
147	      nextSubstate?.batchVideoFrames ?? nextDefaults.frames,
148	      nextSpec,
149	      {
150	        smoothContinuations: currentSettings.smoothContinuations ?? false,
151	        requestedExecutionMode: currentSettings.generationTypeMode ?? 'i2v',
152	      },
153	    );
154	
155	    shotSettingsRef.current.updateFields({
156	      selectedModel: nextModel,
157	      batchVideoFrames: nextFrames,
158	      batchVideoSteps: nextSubstate?.batchVideoSteps ?? nextDefaults.steps,
159	      guidanceScale: nextSubstate?.guidanceScale ?? nextDefaults.guidanceScale,
160	      modelSettingsByModel: {
161	        ...modelSettingsByModel,
162	        [nextModel]: {
163	          batchVideoFrames: nextFrames,
164	          batchVideoSteps: nextSubstate?.batchVideoSteps ?? nextDefaults.steps,
165	          guidanceScale: nextSubstate?.guidanceScale ?? nextDefaults.guidanceScale,
166	        },
167	      },
168	      ...(!nextSpec.ui.turboMode
169	        ? {
170	          turboMode: false,
171	          motionMode: 'basic',
172	          advancedMode: false,
173	        }
174	        : {}),
175	    });
176	  }, []);
177	
178	  useEffect(() => {
179	    const currentSettings = shotSettings.settings;
180	    const currentModel = coerceSelectedModel(currentSettings.selectedModel);
181	    const spec = getModelSpec(currentModel);
182	    const requestedExecutionMode = currentSettings.generationTypeMode ?? 'i2v';
183	    const nextSmoothContinuations = currentSettings.smoothContinuations
184	      && resolveGenerationPolicy(spec, {
185	        smoothContinuations: true,
186	        requestedExecutionMode,
187	      }).continuation.enabled;
188	    const normalizedFrames = clampFrameCountToPolicy(
189	      currentSettings.batchVideoFrames ?? MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel].frames,
190	      spec,
191	      {
192	        smoothContinuations: nextSmoothContinuations,
193	        requestedExecutionMode,
194	      },
195	    );
196	    const currentSubstate = currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel?.[currentModel];
197	    const needsSmoothReset = (currentSettings.smoothContinuations ?? false) !== nextSmoothContinuations;
198	    const needsFrameReset = currentSettings.batchVideoFrames !== normalizedFrames
199	      || currentSubstate?.batchVideoFrames !== normalizedFrames;
200	
201	    if (!needsSmoothReset && !needsFrameReset) {
202	      return;
203	    }
204	
205	    shotSettings.updateFields({
206	      ...(needsSmoothReset ? { smoothContinuations: nextSmoothContinuations } : {}),
207	      ...(needsFrameReset
208	        ? {
209	          batchVideoFrames: normalizedFrames,
210	          modelSettingsByModel: {
211	            ...(currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel ?? {}),
212	            [currentModel]: {
213	              ...currentSubstate,
214	              batchVideoFrames: normalizedFrames,
215	            },
216	          },
217	        }
218	        : {}),
219	    });
220	  }, [
221	    shotSettings.settings.batchVideoFrames,
222	    shotSettings.settings.selectedModel,
223	    shotSettings.settings.generationTypeMode,
224	    shotSettings.settings.smoothContinuations,
225	    shotSettings.settings.modelSettingsByModel,
226	    shotSettings.updateFields,
227	  ]);
228	
229	  const setGuidanceScale = useCallback((guidanceScale: number) => {
230	    const currentSettings = shotSettingsRef.current.settings;
231	    const currentModel = coerceSelectedModel(currentSettings.selectedModel);
232	
233	    shotSettingsRef.current.updateFields({
234	      guidanceScale,
235	      modelSettingsByModel: {
236	        ...(currentSettings.modelSettingsByModel ?? {}),
237	        [currentModel]: {
238	          batchVideoFrames: currentSettings.batchVideoFrames ?? MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel].frames,
239	          batchVideoSteps: currentSettings.batchVideoSteps ?? MODEL_DEFAULTS[currentModel].steps,
240	          guidanceScale,
241	        },
242	      },
243	    });
244	  }, []);
245	
246	  const providerHandlers = useMemo<VideoTravelSettingsHandlers>(() => ({
247	    ...handlers,
248	    handleSelectedModelChange: setSelectedModel,
249	    handleGuidanceScaleChange: setGuidanceScale,
250	  }), [handlers, setGuidanceScale, setSelectedModel]);

235	        batchVideoSteps: MODEL_DEFAULTS['ltx-2.3'].steps,
236	        guidanceScale: MODEL_DEFAULTS['ltx-2.3'].guidanceScale,
237	      },
238	      'ltx-2.3-fast': {
239	        batchVideoFrames: MODEL_DEFAULTS['ltx-2.3-fast'].frames,
240	        batchVideoSteps: MODEL_DEFAULTS['ltx-2.3-fast'].steps,
241	        guidanceScale: MODEL_DEFAULTS['ltx-2.3-fast'].guidanceScale,
242	      },
243	    },
244	  };
245	}
246	
247	function normalizeShotLoras(value: unknown): ShotLora[] {
248	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
249	    return [];
250	  }
251	  return value.flatMap((entry) => {
252	    const record = asRecord(entry);
253	    if (!record) {
254	      return [];
255	    }
256	    const id = asString(record.id);
257	    const name = asString(record.name);
258	    const path = asString(record.path);
259	    const strength = asFiniteNumber(record.strength);
260	    if (!id || !name || !path || strength === undefined) {
261	      return [];
262	    }
263	    return [{
264	      id,
265	      name,
266	      path,
267	      strength,
268	      ...(asString(record.previewImageUrl) ? { previewImageUrl: asString(record.previewImageUrl) } : {}),
269	      ...(asString(record.trigger_word) ? { trigger_word: asString(record.trigger_word) } : {}),
270	    }];
271	  });
272	}
273	
274	function normalizePairConfigs(value: unknown): VideoTravelSettings['pairConfigs'] {
275	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
276	    return [];
277	  }
278	  return value.flatMap((entry) => {
279	    const record = asRecord(entry);
280	    if (!record) {
281	      return [];
282	    }
283	    const id = asString(record.id);
284	    if (!id) {
285	      return [];
286	    }
287	    return [{
288	      id,
289	      prompt: asString(record.prompt) ?? '',
290	      frames: asFiniteNumber(record.frames) ?? videoTravelSettings.defaults.batchVideoFrames,
291	      negativePrompt: asString(record.negativePrompt) ?? '',
292	      context: asFiniteNumber(record.context) ?? 0,
293	    }];
294	  });
295	}
296	
297	function normalizeShotImageIds(value: unknown): string[] {
298	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
299	    return [];
300	  }

150	      selectedPhasePresetId: motion.selectedPhasePresetId,
151	    },
152	    model: {

154	      selectedModel: motion.selectedModel,
155	      numInferenceSteps: motion.batchVideoSteps,
156	      guidanceScale: motion.guidanceScale,
157	      randomSeed: motion.randomSeed,
158	      turboMode: motion.turboMode,
159	      generationTypeMode: motion.generationTypeMode,
160	      smoothContinuations: motion.smoothContinuations,
161	      ltxHdResolution: motion.ltxHdResolution,
162	    },
163	    batchVideoFrames: motion.batchVideoFrames,
164	    selectedLoras: motion.selectedLoras,
165	    travelGuidance: motion.travelGuidance,
166	    structureGuidance: motion.structureGuidance,
167	    structureVideos: motion.structureVideos,

169	    stitchAfterGenerate: buildStitchAfterGenerateConfig(join),
170	  };
171	}
172	
173	export function useGenerationController({
174	  core,
175	  prompt,
176	  motion,
177	  join,
178	  runtime,
179	}: UseGenerationControllerParams) {
180	  const { clearAllEnhancedPrompts, updatePairPromptsByIndex, refetch: loadPositions } = useTimelineCore(core.selectedShotId);
181	  const { onPromptChange } = prompt;
182	  const previousSmoothContinuationsRef = useRef(motion.smoothContinuations);
183	
184	  useEffect(() => {
185	    const wasSmoothContinuationsEnabled = previousSmoothContinuationsRef.current;
186	    if (!wasSmoothContinuationsEnabled && motion.smoothContinuations) {
187	      join.joinSettings?.updateField('stitchAfterGenerate', true);
188	    }
189	    previousSmoothContinuationsRef.current = motion.smoothContinuations;
190	  }, [join.joinSettings, motion.smoothContinuations]);
191	
192	  const handleBatchVideoPromptChangeWithClear = useCallback(async (newPrompt: string) => {
193	    onPromptChange(newPrompt);
194	    try {
195	      await clearAllEnhancedPrompts();
196	    } catch (error) {
197	      normalizeAndPresentError(error, { context: 'PromptClearLog', showToast: false });
198	    }
199	  }, [clearAllEnhancedPrompts, onPromptChange]);
