- **Phase 3:** `guidance_scale: 1.0`, lightx2v low_noise

**Duplicate Definition in vaceDefaults.ts:**
`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/lib/vaceDefaults.ts:8-41`

There are **TWO different DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG definitions**:
1. In `phaseConfig.ts` (lines 86-125): `steps_per_phase: [2, 2, 2]`
2. In `vaceDefaults.ts` (lines 8-41): `steps_per_phase: [2, 2, 5]` (different!)

The test file `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/lib/__tests__/vaceDefaults.test.ts` verifies the `vaceDefaults.ts` version has `[2, 2, 5]` steps.

**HIGH NOISE MULTIPLIER:** Both versions have `high_noise_model.safetensors multiplier: "0.75"` and additional motion_scale_000006500_high_noise.safetensors at multiplier 1.25 (in vaceDefaults only).

---

### **2. Other Builtin Presets**

**I2V Default Preset:**
`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/types/phaseConfig.ts:53-81`
- ID: `__builtin_default_i2v__`
- `num_phases: 2`
- `steps_per_phase: [3, 3]` ✓ (matches your "fine" variant)
- `model_switch_phase: 1`
- **Phase 1:** `guidance_scale: 1.0` (NOT 3), ComfyUI-repackaged LoRA, high_noise multiplier **1.2** ✓ (matches "fine")
- **Phase 2:** `guidance_scale: 1.0`, ComfyUI-repackaged LoRA, low_noise

**Featured Preset IDs:**
- VACE: `d72377eb-6d57-4af1-80a3-9b629da28a47` (Line 53 in segmentSettingsUtils.ts)
- I2V: `e1aad8bf-add9-4d7b-883b-d67d424028c4`, `18b879a5-1251-41dc-b263-613358ced541`

**No other builtin presets match the "burned" config exactly** - it has phase 1 guidance=3 (matching VACE default) and 3 phases with [2,2,2] steps (but stored in vaceDefaults as [2,2,5]!).

---

### **3. VACE vs I2V Mode Switching**

**Mode Detection Logic:**
`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/segmentSettingsUtils.ts:78-81`
```typescript
export function detectGenerationMode(modelName?: string): 'i2v' | 'vace' {
  if (!modelName) return 'i2v';
  return modelName.toLowerCase().includes('vace') ? 'vace' : 'i2v';
}
```

**Preset Auto-Selection on Mode Change:**
`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/usePresetAutoSelect.ts:64-110`

When `generationTypeMode` changes (I2V ↔ VACE):
- **Only in basic mode** - advanced mode skips auto-select (line 73)
- Auto-selects the appropriate builtin preset (line 101-110)
- Tracks `prevGenerationTypeModeRef` to detect mode changes
- Calls `onPhasePresetSelect(builtinDefaultPreset.id, builtinDefaultPreset.metadata.phaseConfig, builtinDefaultPreset.metadata)`

**Critical Issue:** When mode changes, the preset ID is set BUT the phaseConfig should match the builtin preset. However, if user already has a phaseConfig with custom values, the auto-select may create a mismatch.

---

### **4. Model Switching and Phase Config Handling**

**Model Change Handler:**
`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/hooks/useModelChange.ts:10-53`

When switching models:
- Line 41-45: **If switching to a model that doesn't support phaseConfig:**
  ```typescript
  if (currentSpec.supportsPhaseConfig && !nextSpec.supportsPhaseConfig) {
    updates.phaseConfig = undefined;
    updates.selectedPhasePresetId = null;
    updates.motionMode = 'basic';
  }
  ```
- LoRAs are cleared if `loraFamily` changes (line 36-38)

---

### **5. Payload Construction and Preset Resolution**

**Request Body Builder:**
`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideo/requestBody.ts:32-141`

- Line 83-84: Both `phaseConfig` AND `selectedPhasePresetId` are passed to `buildMotionTaskFields()`
- Line 123: `selected_phase_preset_id` is included in payload if set

**Motion Task Fields Builder:**
`/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/SegmentSettingsForm/segmentSettingsUtils.ts:110-129`

```typescript
export function buildMotionTaskFields({
  amountOfMotion,
  motionMode,
  phaseConfig,
  selectedPhasePresetId,
  omitBasicPhaseConfig = false,
}: MotionTaskFieldsInput): Record<string, unknown> {
  const shouldIncludePhaseConfig = !omitBasicPhaseConfig || motionMode !== 'basic' || !!selectedPhasePresetId;

  if (!phaseConfig) {
    return {};
  }

  return {
    amount_of_motion: amountOfMotion / 100,
    motion_mode: motionMode,
    phase_config: shouldIncludePhaseConfig ? stripModeFromPhaseConfig(phaseConfig) : undefined,
    selected_phase_preset_id: selectedPhasePresetId || undefined,
  };
}
```

**KEY INSIGHT:** Both `phase_config` (the actual config) AND `selected_phase_preset_id` (the preset ID) are sent to the backend. The preset ID tells the backend which preset was selected, but the actual phaseConfig is also sent.

---

### **6. Paths Where Mixing Could Happen**

**Critical Code Paths:**

1. **Preset Selection → Phase Config Application:**
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/shared/components/MotionPresetSelector/MotionPresetSelector.tsx:135-140`
   - When a preset is selected, both `preset.id` and `preset.metadata.phaseConfig` are passed

2. **Mode Switch without Preset Sync:**
   - If `generationTypeMode` changes but `motionMode` is 'advanced', auto-select is SKIPPED (usePresetAutoSelect.ts:73)
   - User's manual phaseConfig is preserved, but selectedPhasePresetId may still be the old preset ID
   - Result: Mismatch between selectedPhasePresetId and actual phaseConfig

3. **Advanced Mode Editing:**
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/applySettings/generationSettingsService.ts:144-176`
   - When applying advanced settings, phaseConfig and selectedPhasePresetId are updated independently
   - If only one is updated, they can get out of sync

4. **Task Data Service (Apply Settings):**
   - `/Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/applySettings/taskDataService.ts:157-158`
   - Extracts both `phase_config` and `selected_phase_preset_id` from orchestrator/params
   - No validation that they're consistent

---

### **7. The Duplicate Config Problem**

**TWO definitions of DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG with different step values:**

1. **`phaseConfig.ts`** (shared across tools): `steps_per_phase: [2, 2, 2]`
2. **`vaceDefaults.ts`** (join-clips/edit-video specific): `steps_per_phase: [2, 2, 5]` with extra motion_scale LoRAs

This is a major red flag! Depending on which file is imported, different defaults are used.

**Import locations:**
- `vaceDefaults.ts` imported by: join-clips, edit-video, other VACE tools
- `phaseConfig.ts` imported by: travel-between-images, segment settings, general phase config handling

---

### **8. Summary of Root Causes**

1. **Duplicate DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG** with different `steps_per_phase` values - creates inconsistency
2. **Preset ID stored separately from phaseConfig** - they can get out of sync during mode switches
3. **Advanced mode auto-select is skipped** - leaving selectedPhasePresetId and phaseConfig mismatched
4. **No validation** that selectedPhasePresetId matches the actual phaseConfig being used
5. **Two different high_noise multipliers** in the configs (0.75 vs 1.2 depending on I2V vs VACE)

Your "fine" variant (2 phases, [3,3] steps, guidance 1, ComfyUI LoRAs, multiplier 1.2) matches the I2V default but is marked with `__builtin_default_vace__`.

Your "burned" variant (3 phases, [2,2,2] steps, guidance 3 in phase 1, lightx2v LoRAs, multiplier 0.75) matches the vaceDefaults.ts config but stored with `__builtin_default_vace__`.

This suggests both configs were incorrectly labeled with the same preset ID, or there's confusion about which DEFAULT_VACE_PHASE_CONFIG version is in use.

> DEVELOPER

You are the autonomous megaplan runner for this single run.

Project: /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app
Idea: Refactor the phase config mode-switching architecture in reigh-app to eliminate the dual-writer problem. Currently when generationTypeMode changes (I2V <-> VACE), two mechanisms compete to set phaseConfig: (1) rebuildPhaseConfig called imperatively from handlers, and (2) usePresetAutoSelect firing on next render. This creates race conditions and allows selectedPhasePresetId and phaseConfig to get out of sync. The fix: make handleGenerationTypeModeChange atomically set mode + preset ID + phaseConfig in a single updateFields call using the builtin preset config (with user loras merged in). Remove the redundant rebuildPhaseConfig call from mode switching. Restrict usePresetAutoSelect to only handle initial mount / shot navigation, not mode changes already handled by the explicit handler. Key files: reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers.ts, reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/usePresetAutoSelect.ts, reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideo/modelPhase.ts, reigh-app/src/shared/types/phaseConfig.ts
Execution mode: auto-approve
Robustness: light

## 1. Role & Mission
Your job is to drive the megaplan workflow through the CLI until the run finishes or a defined breakpoint requires the outer conversation.

Always follow these priorities, in order:
1. The latest user direction relayed through notes or resume messages.
2. The live CLI state from `megaplan status --plan <name>`.
3. The workflow and breakpoint rules in this template.
4. Your own memory of earlier turns.

Always do these things:
- Operate through the `megaplan` CLI only. Do not call workers or agents directly.
- Keep the outer conversation clean. Do not ask for routine confirmation.
- Use `next_step` and `valid_next` for routing. If memory and CLI state disagree, trust CLI state.
- Treat user notes as authoritative.

Never do these things:
- Do not run the workflow manually outside the CLI.
- Do not skip required phases for the selected robustness level.
- Do not emit a breakpoint unless one of the breakpoint rules below says to.

## 2. Startup
Start the run like this:
1. The launcher is: `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 megaplan`
2. auto_approve is explicitly configured, so use empty string for auto-approve flag.
3. robustness is NOT explicitly configured, so use `--robustness light`.
4. Run `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 megaplan init --project-dir [REDACTED] --robustness light "Refactor the phase config mode-switching architecture to eliminate the dual-writer problem. Currently when generationTypeMode changes (I2V <-> VACE), two mechanisms compete to set phaseConfig: (1) rebuildPhaseConfig called imperatively from handlers, and (2) usePresetAutoSelect firing on next render. This creates race conditions and allows selectedPhasePresetId and phaseConfig to get out of sync. The fix: make handleGenerationTypeModeChange atomically set mode + preset ID + phaseConfig in a single updateFields call using the builtin preset config (with user loras merged in). Remove the redundant rebuildPhaseConfig call from mode switching. Restrict usePresetAutoSelect to only handle initial mount / shot navigation, not mode changes already handled by the explicit handler. Key files: src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers.ts, src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/settings/usePresetAutoSelect.ts, src/tools/travel-between-images/components/ShotEditor/services/generateVideo/modelPhase.ts, src/shared/types/phaseConfig.ts"`
5. Capture the returned plan name.
6. Output `PLAN_NAME: <name>` on its own line immediately after init.
7. Run `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 megaplan status --plan <name>`.

## 3. Phase Routing (Light Robustness)
Light robustness loop: `plan → critique → revise → finalize → execute`
- There is no prep, no gate, and no review.
- After the light `revise`, the CLI moves to `gated`, so the next command is `finalize`.
- After `execute`, the CLI will end the run.

## 4. After Every Phase
After every phase command, immediately run:
`PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 megaplan status --plan <name>`

Then:
- Re-read `state`, `next_step`, and `valid_next`.
- If `notes_count > 0`, read the full `notes` array.
- If the phase response and `status` disagree, trust `status`.
- If the next move is unclear, prefer the explicit `valid_next` list.

## 5. Breakpoints
Use a breakpoint only for these cases:
- `PHASE_ESCALATE`: a non-execute phase still fails after retry with `--fresh`.
- `EXECUTE_ESCALATE`: `execute` reaches the no-progress cap or repeated blocking/timeouts.

## 6. Safeguards
Non-execute safeguards:
- If any phase fails or returns unusable output, retry once with `--fresh`.
- If the exact same error appears twice, the next retry must use `--fresh`.
- If still failing after fresh retry, stop with `BREAKPOINT: PHASE_ESCALATE`.

Execute safeguards:
- Count whether each `execute` call creates forward progress.
- If 3 consecutive attempts without progress, stop with `BREAKPOINT: EXECUTE_ESCALATE`.

## 7. Execution Details
- After finalize, run `PYENV_VERSION=3.11.11 megaplan execute --plan <name> --confirm-destructive`.
- If per-batch execution is indicated, use `--batch N` and `progress` between batches.

## 8. Completion Format
When done, return exactly:
`COMPLETE: megaplan run finished`
`Plan: <name>`
`Final state: <state>`
`Summary: <outcome>`
`Artifacts: <key files or reports>`
`Follow-up: <only if something remains>`

## IMPORTANT CONTEXT
A partial fix has already been applied to the codebase:
1. `buildBasicModeGenerationRequest` in modelPhase.ts already has a `useVaceModel = false` parameter added
2. `rebuildPhaseConfig` in useVideoTravelSettingsHandlers.ts already reads generationTypeMode and passes it
3. `handleMotionModeChange` already passes useVace
4. `handleRestoreDefaults` already passes the mode

The megaplan should build on this existing work and complete the architectural cleanup: making mode switches atomic, removing the redundant rebuildPhaseConfig call from handleGenerationTypeModeChange, and restricting usePresetAutoSelect scope.
